- **Role:** Transformer-Overhaul Specialist
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for High-Voltage AC Motor Stator Rewinding & Resin VPI Impregnator
- **Intro:** Transformer-Overhaul Specialist — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for High-Voltage AC Motor Stator Rewinding & Resin VPI Impregnator — Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) and Zero-Energy Verification: Voltage must read 0 V AC ± 0.5 V on all phases. Lockout hasps must be rated for 10 kN tensile strength.
